අංංක 2473/19 - 2026 ජනවාාරි මස 29 වැැනි බ්‍රහස්පතින්දාා - 2026.01.29No. 2473/19 - THURSDAY, JANUARY 29, 2026

ARCHITECTS REGISTRATION BOARD

REFERENCE is made to the notes appearing on pages 1A, 2A, 3A, 4A and 5A of the Gazette (Extraordinary) No. 1015/19 of Friday, the 20th February, 1998 of the Democratic Socialist Republic of Sri Lanka with regard to the Registration of persons who are eligible to practice Architecture in Sri Lanka under the Categories "Chartered Architects" ''Architects'' and "Architectural Licentiates". The interpretations of the Sri Lanka Institute of Architects (Amendment) Act, No. 14 of 1966 in the above Gazette Notification will apply in all respects to the list published below.

The Architects Registration Board as per the functions and powers vested upon it by the Section 8B of the Sri Lanka Institute of Architects (Amendment) Act, No. 14 of 1996 has directed me to publish the Register of Chartered Architects, Architects, Architectural Licentiates and the Institutions the qualifications of which are recognized for the time being for the purpose of registration in compliance with Section 8C(1) and (2) of the Act.

The registration of Chartered Architects, Architects and Architectural Licentiates is valid from 01st February 2026 to 31st January 2027, and its validity beyond 31st January 2027 is contingent upon the payment of the renewal fee.

After this publication, if there are new registrations during the year, such will be included in the Register and can be seen on the updated Register published in the ARB official website : www.arb.lk.

N. W. W. Wijekoon, Registrar.Architects Registration Board, Sri Lanka Institute of Architects, No. 120/7, Vidya Mawatha, Colombo 07,27th January, 2026.
